In [16]:
import pandas as pd
import random

first_names = ["aastha", "deepti", "ishita"]
last_names = ["Sharma", "chauhan", "bansal"]
cities = ["Ynr", "Ynr", "kaithal"]

data = []

for _ in range(10000):
    name = f"{random.choice(first_names)} {random.choice(last_names)}"
    age = random.randint(18, 19)
    city = random.choice(cities)

    data.append({
        "Name": name,
        "Age": age,
        "City": city
    })

df = pd.DataFrame(data)

print(df)

                Name  Age     City
0     deepti chauhan   19      Ynr
1     ishita chauhan   19      Ynr
2      ishita bansal   18  kaithal
3      ishita Sharma   18      Ynr
4      ishita bansal   19  kaithal
...              ...  ...      ...
9995   aastha Sharma   18      Ynr
9996   deepti bansal   18      Ynr
9997  ishita chauhan   19      Ynr
9998  aastha chauhan   18      Ynr
9999   aastha bansal   19      Ynr

[10000 rows x 3 columns]
